Note: NMI does NOT use Standard Webhooks — the Webhook-Signature header is "t=&lt;nonce&gt;,s=&lt;sig&gt;" (comma-separated) where t is a NONCE (not a Unix timestamp), and the signature is HMAC-SHA256 over "&lt;nonce&gt;.&lt;raw_body&gt;", lowercase hex.
